Showroom Strategy

Turnover and Replenishment

Prioritize plumbing, electrical, painting, finishing and compact accessories with recurring demand and easy replenishment.

Technical Product Lines

Display energy, metering, ventilation, shower and machinery solutions by specification, voltage, power and application.

Digital Catalog for Bulky Items

Keep doors, windows, shower enclosures, furniture, flooring and heavy machinery in a digital catalog, supported by material and finish samples.

Recommended Showroom Architecture

Bathroom and Metal FixturesSinks, showers, mixers, urinals, shower enclosures and accessories.
KitchenConventional and electric faucets, mixers and compact furniture.
Electrical and EnergyCables, outlets, timers, meters, transformers, solar solutions and generators.
Construction and FinishingMachinery, materials, flooring, grout, painting, openings and ventilation.

Display Priorities

Products with the best balance of demand, recurrence, average ticket and display feasibility.

Methodology

Market size uses only the monthly Overview tab from each file. Listings, Catalog, Brands, Models and Sellers are independent ranking views and are never added together. Commercial product lines group equivalent listings within each subcategory from September 2025 to August 2026. September 2026 is partial and is not included in the 12-month totals. Faucet files marked with “(1)” were classified as kitchen; the remaining faucet files were classified as bathroom. Priority combines captured sales, units, recurrence, average ticket and logistics feasibility for import and display.

Recommended Use

Physically display compact Priority A products. Use technical samples for higher-value items and a digital catalog for bulky products. Before purchasing, validate NCM classification, administrative requirements, applicable Inmetro, Anatel or sector-specific rules, voltage, plug standard, battery and fuel transport, packaging, MOQ and landed cost in Brazil.
